\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eType \u003ccode\u003e/graphify\u003c/code\u003e in your AI coding assistant and it maps your entire project — code, docs, PDFs, images, videos — into a knowledge graph you can query instead of grepping through files.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWorks in Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Kilo Code, Cursor, Gemini CLI, GitHub Copilot CLI, VS Code Copilot Chat, Aider, Amp, OpenClaw, Factory Droid, Trae, Hermes, Kimi Code, Kiro, Pi, Devin CLI, and Google Antigravity.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"/graphify .\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/graphify .\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThat's it. You get three files:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"graphify-out/\n├── graph.html       open in any browser — click nodes, filter, search\n├── GRAPH_REPORT.md  the highlights: key concepts, surprising connections, suggested questions\n└── graph.json       the full graph — query it anytime without re-reading your files\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/\n├── graph.html       open in any browser — click nodes, filter, search\n├── 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOfficial package:\u003c/strong\u003e The PyPI package is \u003ccode\u003egraphifyy\u003c/code\u003e (double-y). Other \u003ccode\u003egraphify*\u003c/code\u003e packages on PyPI are not affiliated. The CLI command is still \u003ccode\u003egraphify\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStep 1 — install the package:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Recommended (uv puts graphify on PATH automatically):\nuv tool install graphifyy\n\n# Alternatives:\npipx install graphifyy\npip install graphifyy  # may need PATH setup — see note below\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Recommended (uv puts graphify on PATH automatically):\u003c/span\u003e\nuv tool install graphifyy\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Alternatives:\u003c/span\u003e\npipx install graphifyy\npip install graphifyy  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e may need PATH setup — see note below\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStep 2 — register the skill with your AI assistant:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"graphify install\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egraphify install\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThat's it. Open your AI assistant and type \u003ccode\u003e/graphify .\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo install the assistant skill into the current repository instead of your user\nprofile, add \u003ccode\u003e--project\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"graphify install --project\ngraphify install --project --platform codex\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egraphify install --project\ngraphify install --project --platform codex\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eProject-scoped installs write under the current directory, for example\n\u003ccode\u003e.claude/skills/graphify/SKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e.agents/skills/graphify/SKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e (plus a\n\u003ccode\u003ereferences/\u003c/code\u003e sidecar the skill loads on demand), and\nprint a \u003ccode\u003egit add\u003c/code\u003e hint for files that can be committed.\nPer-platform commands that support project-scoped installs accept the same flag,\nfor example \u003ccode\u003egraphify claude install --project\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003egraphify codex install --project\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePowerShell note:\u003c/strong\u003e Use \u003ccode\u003egraphify .\u003c/code\u003e not \u003ccode\u003e/graphify .\u003c/code\u003e — the leading slash is a path separator in PowerShell.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify: command not found\u003c/code\u003e?\u003c/strong\u003e Use \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003epipx install graphifyy\u003c/code\u003e — both put the CLI on PATH automatically. With plain \u003ccode\u003epip\u003c/code\u003e, add \u003ccode\u003e~/.local/bin\u003c/code\u003e (Linux) or \u003ccode\u003e~/Library/Python/3.x/bin\u003c/code\u003e (Mac) to your PATH, or run \u003ccode\u003epython -m graphify\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAvoid \u003ccode\u003epip install\u003c/code\u003e on Mac/Windows\u003c/strong\u003e if possible. The skill resolves Python at runtime from \u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/.graphify_python\u003c/code\u003e; if that points to a different environment than where \u003ccode\u003epip\u003c/code\u003e installed the package, you'll get \u003ccode\u003eModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'graphify'\u003c/code\u003e. \u003ccode\u003euv tool install\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003epipx install\u003c/code\u003e isolate the package in their own env and avoid this entirely.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGit hooks and uv tool / pipx:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003egraphify hook install\u003c/code\u003e embeds the current interpreter path directly into the hook scripts at install time, so the post-commit hook fires correctly even in GUI git clients and CI runners where \u003ccode\u003e~/.local/bin\u003c/code\u003e is not on PATH. If you reinstall or upgrade graphify, re-run \u003ccode\u003egraphify hook install\u003c/code\u003e to refresh the embedded path.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eClaude Code (Windows)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify install\u003c/code\u003e (auto-detected) or \u003ccode\u003egraphify install --platform windows\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCodeBuddy\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify install --platform install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKiro IDE/CLI\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify kiro install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePi coding agent\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify install --platform pi\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCursor\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify cursor install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDevin CLI\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify devin install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGoogle Antigravity\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify antigravity install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCodex users also need \u003ccode\u003emulti_agent = true\u003c/code\u003e under \u003ccode\u003e[features]\u003c/code\u003e in \u003ccode\u003e~/.codex/config.toml\u003c/code\u003e for parallel extraction. CodeBuddy uses the same Agent tool and PreToolUse hook mechanism as Claude Code. Factory Droid uses the \u003ccode\u003eTask\u003c/code\u003e tool for parallel subagent dispatch. OpenClaw and Aider use sequential extraction (parallel agent support is still early on those platforms). Trae uses the Agent tool for parallel subagent dispatch and does \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e support PreToolUse hooks — AGENTS.md is the always-on mechanism.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCodex uses \u003ccode\u003e$graphify\u003c/code\u003e instead of \u003ccode\u003e/graphify\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" (faster-whisper + yt-dlp)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euv tool install support\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euv tool install export\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euv tool install APIs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euv tool install (\u003ccode\u003e--backend claude\u003c/code\u003e, uses \u003ccode\u003eANTHROPIC_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euv tool install \"graphifyy[anthropic]\"\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebedrock\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAWS Bedrock (uses IAM, \u003ccode\u003eAZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euv tool install (\u003ccode\u003e--postgres DSN\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euv tool install \"graphifyy[postgres]\"\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003edm\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBYOND DreamMaker install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eVS Code Copilot Chat\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify vscode install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAider\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify aider install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOpenClaw\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify claw install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFactory Droid\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify droid install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTrae\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify trae install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTrae CN\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify trae-cn install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCursor\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify cursor install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGemini CLI\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify gemini install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHermes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify hermes install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKimi Code\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify install --platform kimi\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAmp\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify amp install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKiro IDE/CLI\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify kiro install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePi coding agent\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify pi install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDevin CLI\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify devin install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGoogle Antigravity\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify antigravity install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis writes a small config file that tells your assistant to consult the knowledge graph for codebase questions — preferring scoped queries like \u003ccode\u003egraphify query \"\u0026lt;question\u0026gt;\"\u003c/code\u003e over reading the full report or grepping raw files. On platforms that support payload-bearing hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI), a hook fires automatically before search-style tool calls (and, on Claude Code, before reading source files one by one via the Read/Glob tools) and nudges your assistant toward the graph path. On the others (Codex, OpenCode, Cursor, etc.), the persistent instruction files (\u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.cursor/rules/\u003c/code\u003e, etc.) provide the same query-first guidance. \u003ccode\u003eGRAPH_REPORT.md\u003c/code\u003e is still available for broad architecture review.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCodeBuddy\u003c/strong\u003e does the same two things as Claude Code: writes a \u003ccode\u003eCODEBUDDY.md\u003c/code\u003e section telling CodeBuddy to read \u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/GRAPH_REPORT.md\u003c/code\u003e before answering architecture questions, and installs \u003cstrong\u003ePreToolUse hooks\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e.codebuddy/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e) that fire before Bash search commands and file reads, nudging toward \u003ccode\u003egraphify query\u003c/code\u003e instead.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCodex\u003c/strong\u003e writes to \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e and also installs a \u003cstrong\u003ePreToolUse hook\u003c/strong\u003e in \u003ccode\u003e.codex/hooks.json\u003c/code\u003e that fires before every Bash tool call — same always-on mechanism as Claude Code.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo remove graphify from all platforms at once: \u003ccode\u003egraphify uninstall\u003c/code\u003e (add \u003ccode\u003e--purge\u003c/code\u003e to also delete \u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/\u003c/code\u003e). Or use the per-platform command (e.g. \u003ccode\u003egraphify claude uninstall\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKilo Code\u003c/strong\u003e installs the Graphify skill to \u003ccode\u003e~/.config/kilo/skills/graphify/SKILL.md\u003c/code\u003e and a native \u003ccode\u003e/graphify\u003c/code\u003e command to \u003ccode\u003e~/.config/kilo/command/graphify.md\u003c/code\u003e. \u003ccode\u003egraphify kilo install\u003c/code\u003e also writes \u003ccode\u003eAGENTS.md\u003c/code\u003e plus a native \u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003etool.execute.before\u003c/code\u003e plugin\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e.kilo/plugins/graphify.js\u003c/code\u003e + \u003ccode\u003e.kilo/kilo.json\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e.kilo/kilo.jsonc\u003c/code\u003e registration) so Kilo gets the same always-on graph reminder behavior through native \u003ccode\u003e.kilo\u003c/code\u003e config.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCursor\u003c/strong\u003e writes \u003ccode\u003e.cursor/rules/graphify.mdc\u003c/code\u003e with \u003ccode\u003ealwaysApply: true\u003c/code\u003e — Cursor includes it in every conversation automatically, no hook needed.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat's in the report\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-whats-in-the-report\" class=\"anchor\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGod nodes\u003c/strong\u003e — the most-connected concepts in your project. Everything flows through these.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSurprising connections\u003c/strong\u003e — links between things that live in different files or modules. Ranked by how unexpected they are.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eThe \"why\"\u003c/strong\u003e — inline comments (\u003ccode\u003e# NOTE:\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e# WHY:\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e# HACK:\u003c/code\u003e), docstrings, and design rationale from docs are extracted as separate nodes linked to the code they explain.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSuggested questions\u003c/strong\u003e — 4–5 questions the graph is uniquely positioned to answer.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eConfidence tags\u003c/strong\u003e — every inferred relationship is marked \u003ccode\u003eEXTRACTED\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eINFERRED\u003c/code\u003e, or \u003ccode\u003eAMBIGUOUS\u003c/code\u003e. You always know what was found vs guessed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" (36 tree-sitter grammars)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.py .ts .js .jsx .tsx .mjs .go .rs .java .c .cpp .h .hpp .rb .cs .kt .scala .php .swift .lua .luau .zig .ps1 .psm1 .ex .exs .m .mm .jl .vue .svelte .astro .groovy .gradle .dart .v .sv .svh .sql .f .f90 .f95 .f03 .f08 .pas .pp .dpr .dpk .lpr .inc .dfm .lfm .lpk .sh .bash .json .dm .dme .dmi .dmm .dmf .sln .slnx .csproj .fsproj .vbproj .razor .cshtml\u003c/code\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e.dm\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003e.dme\u003c/code\u003e requires \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy[dm]\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSalesforce Apex\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.cls .trigger\u003c/code\u003e (regex-based; classes, interfaces, enums, methods, triggers, SOQL/DML edges)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTerraform / HCL\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.tf .tfvars .hcl\u003c/code\u003e (requires \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy[terraform]\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMCP configs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e \u003ccode\u003emcp.json\u003c/code\u003e \u003ccode\u003emcp_servers.json\u003c/code\u003e \u003ccode\u003eclaude_desktop_config.json\u003c/code\u003e — extracts server nodes, package refs, env var requirements\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDocs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.md .mdx .qmd .html .txt .rst .yaml .yml\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOffice\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.docx .xlsx\u003c/code\u003e (requires \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy[office]\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGoogle Workspace\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.gdoc .gsheet .gslides\u003c/code\u003e (opt-in; requires \u003ccode\u003egws\u003c/code\u003e auth and \u003ccode\u003e--google-workspace\u003c/code\u003e; Sheets need \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy[google]\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePDFs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.pdf\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eImages\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.png .jpg .webp .gif\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eVideo / Audio\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.mp4 .mov .mp3 .wav\u003c/code\u003e and more (requires \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy[video]\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eYouTube / URLs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eany video URL (requires \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy[video]\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCode is extracted locally with no API calls (AST via tree-sitter). Everything else goes through your AI assistant's model API.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGoogle Drive for desktop \u003ccode\u003e.gdoc\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e.gsheet\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e.gslides\u003c/code\u003e files are shortcut\npointers, not document content. To include native Google Docs, Sheets, and Slides\nin a headless extraction, install and authenticate the\n\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/googleworkspace/cli\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003egws\u003c/code\u003e CLI\u003c/a\u003e, then run:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"uv tool install \u0026quot;graphifyy[google]\u0026quot;  # needed for Google Sheets table rendering\ngws auth login -s drive\ngraphify extract ./docs --google-workspace\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003euv tool install \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003egraphifyy[google]\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e needed for Google Sheets table rendering\u003c/span\u003e\ngws auth login -s drive\ngraphify extract ./docs --google-workspace\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYou can also set \u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_GOOGLE_WORKSPACE=1\u003c/code\u003e. Graphify exports shortcuts into\n\u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/converted/\u003c/code\u003e as Markdown sidecars, then extracts those files.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"/graphify .                        # build graph for current folder\n/graphify ./docs --update          # re-extract only changed files\n/graphify . --cluster-only         # rerun clustering without re-extracting\n/graphify . --cluster-only --resolution 1.5      # more granular communities\n/graphify . --cluster-only --exclude-hubs 99     # suppress utility super-hubs from god-node rankings\n/graphify . --no-viz               # skip the HTML, just the report + JSON\n/graphify . --wiki                 # build a markdown wiki from the graph\ngraphify export callflow-html      # Mermaid architecture/call-flow HTML (auto-regenerates on every git commit if hook is installed)\n\n/graphify query \u0026quot;what connects auth to the database?\u0026quot;\n/graphify path \u0026quot;UserService\u0026quot; \u0026quot;DatabasePool\u0026quot;\n/graphify explain \u0026quot;RateLimiter\u0026quot;\n\n/graphify add https://arxiv.org/abs/1706.03762   # fetch a paper and add it\n/graphify add \u0026lt;youtube-url\u0026gt;                       # transcribe and add a video\n\ngraphify hook install              # auto-rebuild on git commit\ngraphify merge-graphs a.json b.json              # combine two graphs\n\ngraphify prs                       # PR dashboard: CI state, review status, worktree mapping\ngraphify prs 42                    # deep dive on PR #42 with graph impact\ngraphify prs --triage              # AI ranks your review queue (uses whatever backend is configured)\ngraphify prs --conflicts           # PRs sharing graph communities — merge-order risk\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e/graphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e                        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e build graph for current folder\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify ./docs --update          \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e re-extract only changed files\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --cluster-only         \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e rerun clustering without re-extracting\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --cluster-only --resolution 1.5      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e more granular communities\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --cluster-only --exclude-hubs 99     \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e suppress utility super-hubs from god-node rankings\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --no-viz               \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e skip the HTML, just the report + JSON\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --wiki                 \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e build a markdown wiki from the graph\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eexport\u003c/span\u003e callflow-html      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Mermaid architecture/call-flow HTML (auto-regenerates on every git commit if hook is installed)\u003c/span\u003e\n\n/graphify query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ewhat connects auth to the database?\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify path \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eUserService\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eDatabasePool\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify explain \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eRateLimiter\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n/graphify add https://arxiv.org/abs/1706.03762   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e fetch a paper and add it\u003c/span\u003e\n/graphify add \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003eyoutube-url\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e                       \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e transcribe and add a video\u003c/span\u003e\n\ngraphify hook install              \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e auto-rebuild on git commit\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify merge-graphs a.json b.json              \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e combine two graphs\u003c/span\u003e\n\ngraphify prs                       \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e PR dashboard: CI state, review status, worktree mapping\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify prs 42                    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e deep dive on PR #42 with graph impact\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify prs --triage              \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e AI ranks your review queue (uses whatever backend is configured)\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify prs --conflicts           \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e PRs sharing graph communities — merge-order risk\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee the \u003ca href=\"#full-command-reference\"\u003efull command reference\u003c/a\u003e below.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eIgnoring files\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-ignoring-files\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Ignoring files\" href=\"#ignoring-files\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCreate a \u003ccode\u003e.graphifyignore\u003c/code\u003e in your project root — same syntax as \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e, including \u003ccode\u003e!\u003c/code\u003e negation.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e is respected automatically.\u003c/strong\u003e graphify reads the \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e in each directory. If a \u003ccode\u003e.graphifyignore\u003c/code\u003e is also present, the two are \u003cstrong\u003emerged\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003e.graphifyignore\u003c/code\u003e patterns are evaluated last, so they win on conflicts (including \u003ccode\u003e!\u003c/code\u003e negations). Adding a \u003ccode\u003e.graphifyignore\u003c/code\u003e only ever excludes more; it never re-includes a file your \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e already excluded. Subdirectory scoping works the same way as git — an ignore file only affects its own subtree.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# .graphifyignore\nnode_modules/\ndist/\n*.generated.py\n\n# only index src/, ignore everything else\n*\n!src/\n!src/**\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e# .graphifyignore\nnode_modules/\ndist/\n*.generated.py\n\n# only index src/, ignore everything else\n*\n!src/\n!src/**\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/\u003c/code\u003e is meant to be committed to git so everyone on the team starts with a map.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRecommended \u003ccode\u003e.gitignore\u003c/code\u003e additions:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"graphify-out/cost.json        # local only\n# graphify-out/cache/         # optional: commit for speed, skip to keep repo small\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/cost.json        # local only\n# graphify-out/cache/         # optional: commit for speed, skip to keep repo small\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003emanifest.json\u003c/code\u003e is now portable — keys are stored as relative paths and re-anchored on load, so committing it is safe and avoids a full rebuild on first checkout.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWorkflow:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOne person runs \u003ccode\u003e/graphify .\u003c/code\u003e and commits \u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEveryone pulls — their assistant reads the graph immediately.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun \u003ccode\u003egraphify hook install\u003c/code\u003e to auto-rebuild after each commit (AST only, no API cost). This also sets up a git merge driver so \u003ccode\u003egraph.json\u003c/code\u003e is never left with conflict markers — two devs committing in parallel get their graphs union-merged automatically.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhen docs or papers change, run \u003ccode\u003e/graphify --update\u003c/code\u003e to refresh those nodes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eUsing the graph
