flow\u0026quot;\ngraphify query \u0026quot;what connects DigestAuth to Response?\u0026quot; --graph graphify-out/graph.json\n\n# expose the graph as an MCP server (for repeated tool-call access)\npython -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json\npython -m graphify.serve --graph graphify-out/graph.json  # --graph flag also accepted\n\n# register with Kimi Code:\nkimi mcp add --transport stdio graphify -- python -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json\n\n# or serve over HTTP so a whole team points at one URL (no local graphify needed):\npython -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json --transport http --port 8080\npython -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json --transport http --host 0.0.0.0 --api-key \u0026quot;$SECRET\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e query the graph from the terminal\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eshow the auth flow\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ewhat connects DigestAuth to Response?\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --graph graphify-out/graph.json\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e expose the graph as an MCP server (for repeated tool-call access)\u003c/span\u003e\npython -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json\npython -m graphify.serve --graph graphify-out/graph.json  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e --graph flag also accepted\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e register with Kimi Code:\u003c/span\u003e\nkimi mcp add --transport stdio graphify -- python -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e or serve over HTTP so a whole team points at one URL (no local graphify needed):\u003c/span\u003e\npython -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json --transport http --port 8080\npython -m graphify.serve graphify-out/graph.json --transport http --host 0.0.0.0 --api-key \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003e$SECRET\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe MCP server gives your assistant structured access: \u003ccode\u003equery_graph\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eget_node\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eget_neighbors\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eshortest_path\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elist_prs\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eget_pr_impact\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etriage_prs\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eShared HTTP server\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-shared-http-server\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Shared HTTP server\" href=\"#shared-http-server\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--transport stdio\u003c/code\u003e (the default) spawns one local server per developer. \u003ccode\u003e--transport http\u003c/code\u003e serves the same tools over the MCP Streamable HTTP transport, so a single shared process can serve the graph for the whole team — clients point their IDE MCP config at \u003ccode\u003ehttp://\u0026lt;host\u0026gt;:8080/mcp\u003c/code\u003e instead of running graphify locally.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eFlag\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eDefault\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003ePurpose\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--transport {stdio,http}\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003estdio\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTransport to serve on\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--host\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e127.0.0.1\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHTTP bind host (use \u003ccode\u003e0.0.0.0\u003c/code\u003e to expose beyond localhost)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--port\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e8080\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHTTP bind port\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--api-key\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eenv \u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRequire \u003ccode\u003eAuthorization: Bearer \u0026lt;key\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e (or \u003ccode\u003eX-API-Key\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--path\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/mcp\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eHTTP mount path\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--json-response\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoff\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReturn plain JSON instead of SSE streams\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--stateless\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoff\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo per-session state (for load-balanced / CI deployments)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--session-timeout\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e3600\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReap idle stateful sessions after N seconds (\u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e disables)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe default \u003ccode\u003e127.0.0.1\u003c/code\u003e bind is loopback-only. Set \u003ccode\u003e--host 0.0.0.0\u003c/code\u003e \u003cstrong\u003eand\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003e--api-key\u003c/code\u003e together when exposing on a shared host. Run it in a container:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"docker build -t graphify .\ndocker run -p 8080:8080 -v \u0026quot;$(pwd)/graphify-out:/data\u0026quot; graphify \\\n  /data/graph.json --transport http --host 0.0.0.0 --api-key \u0026quot;$SECRET\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003edocker build -t graphify \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\ndocker run -p 8080:8080 -v \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e$(\u003c/span\u003epwd\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e/graphify-out:/data\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e graphify \\\n  /data/graph.json --transport http --host 0.0.0.0 --api-key \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003e$SECRET\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWSL / Linux note:\u003c/strong\u003e Ubuntu ships \u003ccode\u003epython3\u003c/code\u003e, not \u003ccode\u003epython\u003c/code\u003e. Use a venv to avoid conflicts:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"python3 -m venv .venv \u0026amp;\u0026amp; .venv/bin/pip install \u0026quot;graphifyy[mcp]\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epython3 -m venv .venv \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026amp;\u0026amp;\u003c/span\u003e .venv/bin/pip install \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003egraphifyy[mcp]\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eEnvironment variables\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-environment-variables\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Environment variables\" href=\"#environment-variables\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThese are only needed for \u003cstrong\u003eheadless / CI extraction\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003egraphify extract\u003c/code\u003e). When running via the \u003ccode\u003e/graphify\u003c/code\u003e skill inside your IDE, the model API is provided by your IDE session — no extra keys needed.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eVariable\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eUsed for\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhen required\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eANTHROPIC_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eClaude (Anthropic) backend\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend claude\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eANTHROPIC_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAnthropic-compatible endpoint URL (LiteLLM proxy, gateways, ...)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend claude\u003c/code\u003e (default: \u003ccode\u003ehttps://api.anthropic.com\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eANTHROPIC_MODEL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eModel name for the Claude backend — for custom endpoints, use the model name/alias your server exposes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend claude\u003c/code\u003e (default: \u003ccode\u003eclaude-sonnet-4-6\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGEMINI_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003eGOOGLE_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGoogle Gemini backend\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend gemini\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eOPENAI_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOpenAI or OpenAI-compatible APIs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend openai\u003c/code\u003e (local servers accept any non-empty value)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eOPENAI_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOpenAI-compatible server URL (llama.cpp, vLLM, LM Studio, ...)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend openai\u003c/code\u003e (default: \u003ccode\u003ehttps://api.openai.com/v1\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eOPENAI_MODEL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eModel name for the OpenAI backend — for self-hosted servers, use the model name/alias your server exposes (check its \u003ccode\u003e/v1/models\u003c/code\u003e endpoint), e.g. \u003ccode\u003eLFM2.5-8B-A1B-UD-Q4_K_XL\u003c/code\u003e for llama.cpp\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend openai\u003c/code\u003e (default: \u003ccode\u003egpt-4.1-mini\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eDEEPSEEK_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDeepSeek backend\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend deepseek\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eMOONSHOT_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKimi Code backend\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend kimi\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eOLLAMA_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOllama local inference URL\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend ollama\u003c/code\u003e (default: \u003ccode\u003ehttp://localhost:11434\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eOLLAMA_MODEL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOllama model name\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend ollama\u003c/code\u003e (default: auto-detect)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_NUM_CTX\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOverride Ollama KV-cache window size\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — auto-sized by default\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMinutes to keep Ollama model loaded\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — set \u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e to unload after each chunk\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAzure OpenAI Service backend\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend azure\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAzure resource endpoint URL\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend azure\u003c/code\u003e (required alongside API key)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAZURE_OPENAI_API_VERSION\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAzure API version override\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — default \u003ccode\u003e2024-12-01-preview\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_AZURE_MODEL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAzure deployment name\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — default \u003ccode\u003egpt-4o\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAWS_*\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e~/.aws/credentials\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAWS Bedrock — standard credential chain\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--backend bedrock\u003c/code\u003e (no API key, uses IAM)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_MAX_WORKERS\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAST parallelism thread count\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — also \u003ccode\u003e--max-workers\u003c/code\u003e flag\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRaise output cap for dense corpora\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — e.g. \u003ccode\u003e32768\u003c/code\u003e for large files\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_API_TIMEOUT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePer-call timeout in seconds for HTTP, claude-cli, and Anthropic SDK backends (default: 600)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — also \u003ccode\u003e--api-timeout\u003c/code\u003e flag\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_FORCE\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eForce graph rebuild even with fewer nodes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — also \u003ccode\u003e--force\u003c/code\u003e flag\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_GOOGLE_WORKSPACE\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAuto-enable Google Workspace export\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — set to \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_TRIAGE_BACKEND\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBackend for \u003ccode\u003egraphify prs --triage\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — auto-detected from available keys\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_TRIAGE_MODEL\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eModel override for triage\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — e.g. \u003ccode\u003eclaude-opus-4-7\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_QUERY_LOG\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOverride query log path (default: \u003ccode\u003e~/.cache/graphify-queries.log\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — set to empty or \u003ccode\u003e/dev/null\u003c/code\u003e to silence\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_QUERY_LOG_DISABLE\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSet to \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e to disable query logging entirely\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_QUERY_LOG_RESPONSES\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSet to \u003ccode\u003e1\u003c/code\u003e to also log full subgraph responses (off by default)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_MAX_GRAPH_BYTES\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOverride the 512 MiB graph.json size cap — e.g. \u003ccode\u003e700MB\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e2GB\u003c/code\u003e, or plain bytes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — useful for very large corpora\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_LLM_TEMPERATURE\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOverride LLM temperature for semantic extraction — e.g. \u003ccode\u003e0.7\u003c/code\u003e, or \u003ccode\u003enone\u003c/code\u003e to omit\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eoptional — auto-omitted for o1/o3/o4/gpt-5 reasoning models\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrivacy\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-privacy\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Privacy\" href=\"#privacy\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCode files\u003c/strong\u003e — processed locally via tree-sitter. Nothing leaves your machine. A code-only corpus requires no API key — \u003ccode\u003egraphify extract\u003c/code\u003e runs fully offline.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVideo / audio\u003c/strong\u003e — transcribed locally with faster-whisper. Nothing leaves your machine.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDocs, PDFs, images\u003c/strong\u003e — sent to your AI assistant for semantic extraction (via the \u003ccode\u003e/graphify\u003c/code\u003e skill, using whatever model your IDE session runs). Headless \u003ccode\u003egraphify extract\u003c/code\u003e requires \u003ccode\u003eGEMINI_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eGOOGLE_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e (Gemini), \u003ccode\u003eMOONSHOT_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e (Kimi), \u003ccode\u003eANTHROPIC_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e (Claude), \u003ccode\u003eOPENAI_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e (OpenAI), \u003ccode\u003eDEEPSEEK_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e (DeepSeek), a running Ollama instance (\u003ccode\u003eOLLAMA_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e), AWS credentials via the standard provider chain (Bedrock - no API key needed, uses IAM), or the \u003ccode\u003eclaude\u003c/code\u003e CLI binary (Claude Code - no API key needed, uses your Claude subscription). The \u003ccode\u003e--dedup-llm\u003c/code\u003e flag uses the same key.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eData residency\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003egraphify extract\u003c/code\u003e auto-detects which provider to use based on which API key is set (priority: Gemini → Kimi → Claude → OpenAI → DeepSeek → Azure → Bedrock → Ollama). For code with data-residency requirements, use \u003ccode\u003e--backend ollama\u003c/code\u003e (fully local) or pass an explicit \u003ccode\u003e--backend\u003c/code\u003e flag. Kimi (\u003ccode\u003eMOONSHOT_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e) routes to Moonshot AI servers in China.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNo telemetry, no usage tracking, no analytics.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQuery logging\u003c/strong\u003e — every \u003ccode\u003egraphify query\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003egraphify path\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003egraphify explain\u003c/code\u003e, and MCP \u003ccode\u003equery_graph\u003c/code\u003e call is logged to \u003ccode\u003e~/.cache/graphify-queries.log\u003c/code\u003e in JSON Lines format (timestamp, question, corpus, nodes returned, duration). Full subgraph responses are \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e stored by default. Set \u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_QUERY_LOG_DISABLE=1\u003c/code\u003e to opt out, or \u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_QUERY_LOG=/dev/null\u003c/code\u003e to silence without disabling the code path.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eTroubleshooting\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-troubleshooting\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Troubleshooting\" href=\"#troubleshooting\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraphify: command not found\u003c/code\u003e after \u003ccode\u003epip install graphifyy\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\npip installs scripts to a user bin directory that may not be on your PATH. Fix:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003emacOS: add \u003ccode\u003e~/Library/Python/3.x/bin\u003c/code\u003e to your PATH in \u003ccode\u003e~/.zshrc\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLinux: add \u003ccode\u003e~/.local/bin\u003c/code\u003e to your PATH in \u003ccode\u003e~/.bashrc\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOr use \u003ccode\u003euv tool install graphifyy\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003epipx install graphifyy\u003c/code\u003e — both manage PATH automatically.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003epython -m graphify\u003c/code\u003e works but \u003ccode\u003egraphify\u003c/code\u003e command doesn't\u003c/strong\u003e\nYour shell's PATH doesn't include the Python scripts directory. Use \u003ccode\u003euv\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003epipx\u003c/code\u003e instead of plain \u003ccode\u003epip\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/graphify .\u003c/code\u003e causes \"path not recognized\" in PowerShell\u003c/strong\u003e\nPowerShell treats a leading \u003ccode\u003e/\u003c/code\u003e as a path separator. Use \u003ccode\u003egraphify .\u003c/code\u003e (no slash) on Windows.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGraph has fewer nodes after \u003ccode\u003e--update\u003c/code\u003e or rebuild\u003c/strong\u003e\nIf a refactor deleted files, the old nodes linger. Pass \u003ccode\u003e--force\u003c/code\u003e (or set \u003ccode\u003eGRAPHIFY_FORCE=1\u003c/code\u003e) to overwrite even when the rebuild has fewer nodes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGraph has duplicate nodes for the same entity (ghost duplicates)\u003c/strong\u003e\nGhost duplicates (same symbol appearing twice — once from AST extraction with a source location, once from semantic extraction without) are now automatically merged at build time. If you see this in a graph built before v0.8.33, run a full re-extract to clean up:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"graphify extract . --force\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egraphify extract \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --force\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOllama runs out of VRAM / context window exceeded\u003c/strong\u003e\nThe KV-cache window is auto-sized but may be too large for your GPU. Reduce it:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"GRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_NUM_CTX=8192 graphify extract ./docs --backend ollama --token-budget 4000\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003eGRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_NUM_CTX=8192 graphify extract ./docs --backend ollama --token-budget 4000\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eLLM returned invalid JSON\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eUnterminated string\u003c/code\u003e warnings\u003c/strong\u003e\nThe model's JSON response hit its output-token limit and was cut off mid-string. graphify auto-recovers (it splits the chunk and re-extracts the halves, and an oversized single document is first sliced at heading/paragraph boundaries so the whole file is still covered), so these warnings are noisy but not data loss. To reduce the churn, raise the output cap or shrink each chunk's output:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"GRAPHIFY_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS=16384 graphify extract . --mode deep   # lift the cap\ngraphify extract . --mode deep --token-budget 4000                # smaller input chunks -\u0026gt; smaller output\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003eGRAPHIFY_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS=16384 graphify extract \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --mode deep   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e lift the cap\u003c/span\u003e\ngraphify extract \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e --mode deep --token-budget 4000                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e smaller input chunks -\u0026gt; smaller output\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWith a cloud gateway like OpenRouter, prefer \u003ccode\u003e--backend openai\u003c/code\u003e (set \u003ccode\u003eOPENAI_BASE_URL\u003c/code\u003e) over the Ollama shim — it's a cleaner OpenAI-compatible path. If the model has its own max-output ceiling, lowering \u003ccode\u003e--token-budget\u003c/code\u003e is the reliable lever.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGraph HTML is too large to open in a browser (\u0026gt;5000 nodes)\u003c/strong\u003e\nSkip HTML generation and use the JSON directly:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"graphify cluster-only ./my-project --no-viz\ngraphify query \u0026quot;...\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egraphify cluster-only ./my-project --no-viz\ngraphify query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e...\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003egraph.json\u003c/code\u003e has conflict markers after two devs commit at once\u003c/strong\u003e\nRun \u003ccode\u003egraphify hook install\u003c/code\u003e — it sets up a git merge driver that union-merges \u003ccode\u003egraph.json\u003c/code\u003e automatically so conflicts never happen.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExtraction returns empty nodes/edges for docs or PDFs\u003c/strong\u003e\nDocs, PDFs, and images require an LLM call — code-only corpora need no key. Check that your API key is set and the backend is correct:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-... graphify extract ./docs --backend claude\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003eANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-... graphify extract ./docs --backend claude\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSkill version mismatch warning in your IDE\u003c/strong\u003e\nYour installed graphify version is different from the skill file. Update:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"uv tool upgrade graphifyy\ngraphify install  # overwrites the skill file\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003euv tool upgrade graphifyy\ngraphify install  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e overwrites the skill file\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFull command reference\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-full-command-reference\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Full command reference\" href=\"#full-command-reference\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"/graphify                          # run on current directory\n/graphify ./raw                    # run on a specific folder\n/graphify ./raw --mode deep        # more aggressive relationship extraction\n/graphify ./raw --update           # re-extract only changed files\n/graphify ./raw --directed         # preserve edge direction\n/graphify ./raw --cluster-only     # rerun clustering on existing graph\n/graphify ./raw --no-viz           # skip HTML visualization\n/graphify ./raw --obsidian         # generate Obsidian vault\n/graphify ./raw --wiki             # build agent-crawlable markdown wiki\n/graphify ./raw --svg              # export graph.svg\n/graphify ./raw --graphml          # export for Gephi / yEd\n/graphify ./raw --neo4j            # generate cypher.txt for Neo4j\n/graphify ./raw --neo4j-push bolt://localhost:7687\n/graphify ./raw --falkordb         # generate cypher.txt for FalkorDB\n/graphify ./raw --falkordb-push falkordb://localhost:6379\n/graphify ./raw --watch            # auto-sync as files change\n/graphify ./raw --mcp              # start MCP stdio server\n\n/graphify add https://arxiv.org/abs/1706.03762\n/graphify add \u0026lt;video-url\u0026gt;\n/graphify add https://... --author \u0026quot;Name\u0026quot; --contributor \u0026quot;Name\u0026quot;\n\n/graphify query \u0026quot;what connects attention to the optimizer?\u0026quot;\n/graphify query \u0026quot;...\u0026quot; --dfs --budget 1500\n/graphify path \u0026quot;DigestAuth\u0026quot; \u0026quot;Response\u0026quot;\n/graphify explain \u0026quot;SwinTransformer\u0026quot;\n\ngraphify uninstall                 # remove from all platforms in one shot\ngraphify uninstall --purge         # also delete graphify-out/\ngraphify uninstall --project --platform codex  # remove project-scoped install files only\n\ngraphify hook install              # post-commit + post-checkout hooks\ngraphify hook uninstall\ngraphify hook status\n\n# always-on assistant instructions - platform-specific\ngraphify claude install            # CLAUDE.md + PreToolUse hook (Claude Code)\ngraphify claude uninstall\ngraphify codebuddy install         # CODEBUDDY.md + PreToolUse hook (CodeBuddy)\ngraphify codebuddy uninstall\ngraphify codex install             # AGENTS.md + PreToolUse hook in .codex/hooks.json (Codex)\ngraphify opencode install          # AGENTS.md + tool.execute.before plugin (OpenCode)\ngraphify kilo install              # native Kilo skill + /graphify command + AGENTS.md + .kilo plugin\ngraphify kilo uninstall\ngraphify cursor install            # .cursor/rules/graphify.mdc (Cursor)\ngraphify cursor uninstall\ngraphify gemini install            # GEMINI.md + BeforeTool hook (Gemini CLI)\ngraphify gemini uninstall\ngraphify copilot install           # skill file (GitHub Copilot CLI)\ngraphify copilot uninstall\ngraphify aider install             # AGENTS.md (Aider)\ngraphify aider uninstall\ngraphify claw install              # AGENTS.md (OpenClaw)\ngraphify claw uninstall\ngraphify droid install             # AGENTS.md (Factory Droid)\ngraphify droid uninstall\ngraphify trae install              # AGENTS.md (Trae)\ngraphify trae uninstall\ngraphify trae-cn install           # AGENTS.md (Trae CN)\ngraphify trae-cn uninstall\ngraphify hermes install             # AGENTS.md + ~/.hermes/skills/ (Hermes)\ngraphify hermes uninstall\ngraphify amp install               # skill file (Amp)\ngraphify amp uninstall\ngraphify kiro install               # .kiro/skills/ + .kiro/steering/graphify.md (Kiro IDE/CLI)\ngraphify kiro uninstall\ngraphify pi install                # skill file (Pi coding agent)\ngraphify pi uninstall\ngraphify devin install             # skill file + .windsurf/rules/graphify.md (Devin CLI)\ngraphify devin uninstall\ngraphify antigravity install       # .agents/rules + .agents/workflows (Google Antigravity)\ngraphify antigravity uninstall\n\ngraphify extract ./docs                        # headless LLM extraction for CI (no IDE needed)\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend gemini       # explicit backend: gemini, kimi, claude, openai, deepseek, ollama, bedrock, or claude-cli\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend gemini --model gemini-3.1-pro-preview\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend ollama       # local Ollama (set OLLAMA_BASE_URL / OLLAMA_MODEL) - no API key needed for loopback\nOPENAI_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8080/v1 OPENAI_MODEL=my-model graphify extract ./docs --backend openai   # any OpenAI-compatible server (llama.cpp, vLLM, LM Studio)\nANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:4000 ANTHROPIC_MODEL=my-model graphify extract ./docs --backend claude   # any Anthropic-compatible endpoint (LiteLLM proxy, gateways)\nGRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_NUM_CTX=32768 graphify extract ./docs --backend ollama   # override KV-cache window (auto-sized by default)\nGRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE=0 graphify extract ./docs --backend ollama    # unload model after each chunk (saves VRAM on small GPUs)\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend bedrock      # AWS Bedrock via IAM - no API key, uses AWS credential chain\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend claude-cli   # route through Claude Code CLI - no API key, uses your Claude subscription\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend azure        # Azure OpenAI (set AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY + AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT)\ngraphify extract ./docs --max-workers 16       # AST parallelism (also GRAPHIFY_MAX_WORKERS)\ngraphify extract --postgres \u0026quot;postgresql://user:pass@host/db\u0026quot;   # introspect live PostgreSQL schema directly\ngraphify extract ./my-workspace --cargo        # introspect Rust Cargo workspace dependencies directly\ngraphify extract ./docs --token-budget 30000   # smaller semantic chunks for local/small models\ngraphify extract ./docs --max-concurrency 2    # fewer parallel LLM calls (useful for local inference)\ngraphify extract ./docs --api-timeout 900      # longer HTTP timeout for slow local models (default 600s)\ngraphify extract ./docs --google-workspace     # export .gdoc/.gsheet/.gslides via gws before extraction\ngraphify extract ./docs --mode deep            # richer semantic extraction via extended system prompt\ngraphify extract ./docs --no-cluster           # raw extraction only, skip clustering\ngraphify extract ./docs --force                # overwrite graph.json even if new graph has fewer nodes (use after refactors or to clear ghost duplicates)\ngraphify extract ./docs --dedup-llm            # LLM tiebreaker for ambiguous entity pairs (uses same API key)\ngraphify extract ./docs --global --as myrepo   # extract and register into the cross-project global graph\nGRAPHIFY_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS=32768 graphify extract ./docs --backend claude  # raise output cap for dense corpora\n\ngraphify export callflow-html                       # graphify-out/\u0026lt;project\u0026gt;-callflow.html\ngraphify export callflow-html --max-sections 8      # cap generated architecture sections\ngraphify export callflow-html --output docs/arch.html\ngraphify export callflow-html ./some-repo/graphify-out\n\ngraphify global add graphify-out/graph.json myrepo   # register a project graph into ~/.graphify/global.json\ngraphify global remove myrepo                         # remove a project from the global graph\ngraphify global list                                  # show all registered repos + node/edge counts\ngraphify global path                                  # print path to the global graph file\n\ngraphify prs                              # PR dashboard: CI, review, worktree, graph impact\ngraphify prs 42                           # deep dive on PR #42\ngraphify prs --triage                     # AI triage ranking (auto-detects backend from env)\ngraphify prs --worktrees                  # worktree → branch → PR mapping\ngraphify prs --conflicts                  # PRs sharing graph communities (merge-order risk)\ngraphify prs --base main                  # filter to PRs targeting a specific base branch\ngraphify prs --repo owner/repo            # run against a different GitHub repo\nGRAPHIFY_TRIAGE_BACKEND=kimi graphify prs --triage   # use a specific backend for triage\n\ngraphify clone https://github.com/karpathy/nanoGPT\ngraphify merge-graphs a.json b.json --out merged.json\ngraphify --version                                    # print installed version\ngraphify watch ./src\ngraphify check-update ./src\ngraphify update ./src\ngraphify update ./src --no-cluster  # skip reclustering, write raw AST graph only\ngraphify update ./src --force       # overwrite even if new graph has fewer nodes\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --graph path/to/graph.json  # custom graph location\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --resolution 1.5            # more, smaller communities\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --exclude-hubs 99           # exclude p99 degree nodes from partitioning\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --no-label                  # keep \u0026quot;Community N\u0026quot; placeholders\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --backend=gemini            # backend for community naming\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --backend=gemini --model gemini-2.5-pro  # specific model\ngraphify label ./my-project                                    # (re)name communities with the configured backend\ngraphify label ./my-project --backend=openai --model gpt-4o   # force a specific backend and model\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e/graphify                          # run on current directory\n/graphify ./raw                    # run on a specific folder\n/graphify ./raw --mode deep        # more aggressive relationship extraction\n/graphify ./raw --update           # re-extract only changed files\n/graphify ./raw --directed         # preserve edge direction\n/graphify ./raw --cluster-only     # rerun clustering on existing graph\n/graphify ./raw --no-viz           # skip HTML visualization\n/graphify ./raw --obsidian         # generate Obsidian vault\n/graphify ./raw --wiki             # build agent-crawlable markdown wiki\n/graphify ./raw --svg              # export graph.svg\n/graphify ./raw --graphml          # export for Gephi / yEd\n/graphify ./raw --neo4j            # generate cypher.txt for Neo4j\n/graphify ./raw --neo4j-push bolt://localhost:7687\n/graphify ./raw --falkordb         # generate cypher.txt for FalkorDB\n/graphify ./raw --falkordb-push falkordb://localhost:6379\n/graphify ./raw --watch            # auto-sync as files change\n/graphify ./raw --mcp              # start MCP stdio server\n\n/graphify add https://arxiv.org/abs/1706.03762\n/graphify add \u0026lt;video-url\u0026gt;\n/graphify add https://... --author \"Name\" --contributor \"Name\"\n\n/graphify query \"what connects attention to the optimizer?\"\n/graphify query \"...\" --dfs --budget 1500\n/graphify path \"DigestAuth\" \"Response\"\n/graphify explain \"SwinTransformer\"\n\ngraphify uninstall                 # remove from all platforms in one shot\ngraphify uninstall --purge         # also delete graphify-out/\ngraphify uninstall --project --platform codex  # remove project-scoped install files only\n\ngraphify hook install              # post-commit + post-checkout hooks\ngraphify hook uninstall\ngraphify hook status\n\n# always-on assistant instructions - platform-specific\ngraphify claude install            # CLAUDE.md + PreToolUse hook (Claude Code)\ngraphify claude uninstall\ngraphify codebuddy install         # CODEBUDDY.md + PreToolUse hook (CodeBuddy)\ngraphify codebuddy uninstall\ngraphify codex install             # AGENTS.md + PreToolUse hook in .codex/hooks.json (Codex)\ngraphify opencode install          # AGENTS.md + tool.execute.before plugin (OpenCode)\ngraphify kilo install              # native Kilo skill + /graphify command + AGENTS.md + .kilo plugin\ngraphify kilo uninstall\ngraphify cursor install            # .cursor/rules/graphify.mdc (Cursor)\ngraphify cursor uninstall\ngraphify gemini install            # GEMINI.md + BeforeTool hook (Gemini CLI)\ngraphify gemini uninstall\ngraphify copilot install           # skill file (GitHub Copilot CLI)\ngraphify copilot uninstall\ngraphify aider install             # AGENTS.md (Aider)\ngraphify aider uninstall\ngraphify claw install              # AGENTS.md (OpenClaw)\ngraphify claw uninstall\ngraphify droid install             # AGENTS.md (Factory Droid)\ngraphify droid uninstall\ngraphify trae install              # AGENTS.md (Trae)\ngraphify trae uninstall\ngraphify trae-cn install           # AGENTS.md (Trae CN)\ngraphify trae-cn uninstall\ngraphify hermes install             # AGENTS.md + ~/.hermes/skills/ (Hermes)\ngraphify hermes uninstall\ngraphify amp install               # skill file (Amp)\ngraphify amp uninstall\ngraphify kiro install               # .kiro/skills/ + .kiro/steering/graphify.md (Kiro IDE/CLI)\ngraphify kiro uninstall\ngraphify pi install                # skill file (Pi coding agent)\ngraphify pi uninstall\ngraphify devin install             # skill file + .windsurf/rules/graphify.md (Devin CLI)\ngraphify devin uninstall\ngraphify antigravity install       # .agents/rules + .agents/workflows (Google Antigravity)\ngraphify antigravity uninstall\n\ngraphify extract ./docs                        # headless LLM extraction for CI (no IDE needed)\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend gemini       # explicit backend: gemini, kimi, claude, openai, deepseek, ollama, bedrock, or claude-cli\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend gemini --model gemini-3.1-pro-preview\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend ollama       # local Ollama (set OLLAMA_BASE_URL / OLLAMA_MODEL) - no API key needed for loopback\nOPENAI_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8080/v1 OPENAI_MODEL=my-model graphify extract ./docs --backend openai   # any OpenAI-compatible server (llama.cpp, vLLM, LM Studio)\nANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:4000 ANTHROPIC_MODEL=my-model graphify extract ./docs --backend claude   # any Anthropic-compatible endpoint (LiteLLM proxy, gateways)\nGRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_NUM_CTX=32768 graphify extract ./docs --backend ollama   # override KV-cache window (auto-sized by default)\nGRAPHIFY_OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE=0 graphify extract ./docs --backend ollama    # unload model after each chunk (saves VRAM on small GPUs)\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend bedrock      # AWS Bedrock via IAM - no API key, uses AWS credential chain\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend claude-cli   # route through Claude Code CLI - no API key, uses your Claude subscription\ngraphify extract ./docs --backend azure        # Azure OpenAI (set AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY + AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT)\ngraphify extract ./docs --max-workers 16       # AST parallelism (also GRAPHIFY_MAX_WORKERS)\ngraphify extract --postgres \"postgresql://user:pass@host/db\"   # introspect live PostgreSQL schema directly\ngraphify extract ./my-workspace --cargo        # introspect Rust Cargo workspace dependencies directly\ngraphify extract ./docs --token-budget 30000   # smaller semantic chunks for local/small models\ngraphify extract ./docs --max-concurrency 2    # fewer parallel LLM calls (useful for local inference)\ngraphify extract ./docs --api-timeout 900      # longer HTTP timeout for slow local models (default 600s)\ngraphify extract ./docs --google-workspace     # export .gdoc/.gsheet/.gslides via gws before extraction\ngraphify extract ./docs --mode deep            # richer semantic extraction via extended system prompt\ngraphify extract ./docs --no-cluster           # raw extraction only, skip clustering\ngraphify extract ./docs --force                # overwrite graph.json even if new graph has fewer nodes (use after refactors or to clear ghost duplicates)\ngraphify extract ./docs --dedup-llm            # LLM tiebreaker for ambiguous entity pairs (uses same API key)\ngraphify extract ./docs --global --as myrepo   # extract and register into the cross-project global graph\nGRAPHIFY_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS=32768 graphify extract ./docs --backend claude  # raise output cap for dense corpora\n\ngraphify export callflow-html                       # graphify-out/\u0026lt;project\u0026gt;-callflow.html\ngraphify export callflow-html --max-sections 8      # cap generated architecture sections\ngraphify export callflow-html --output docs/arch.html\ngraphify export callflow-html ./some-repo/graphify-out\n\ngraphify global add graphify-out/graph.json myrepo   # register a project graph into ~/.graphify/global.json\ngraphify global remove myrepo                         # remove a project from the global graph\ngraphify global list                                  # show all registered repos + node/edge counts\ngraphify global path                                  # print path to the global graph file\n\ngraphify prs                              # PR dashboard: CI, review, worktree, graph impact\ngraphify prs 42                           # deep dive on PR #42\ngraphify prs --triage                     # AI triage ranking (auto-detects backend from env)\ngraphify prs --worktrees                  # worktree → branch → PR mapping\ngraphify prs --conflicts                  # PRs sharing graph communities (merge-order risk)\ngraphify prs --base main                  # filter to PRs targeting a specific base branch\ngraphify prs --repo owner/repo            # run against a different GitHub repo\nGRAPHIFY_TRIAGE_BACKEND=kimi graphify prs --triage   # use a specific backend for triage\n\ngraphify clone https://github.com/karpathy/nanoGPT\ngraphify merge-graphs a.json b.json --out merged.json\ngraphify --version                                    # print installed version\ngraphify watch ./src\ngraphify check-update ./src\ngraphify update ./src\ngraphify update ./src --no-cluster  # skip reclustering, write raw AST graph only\ngraphify update ./src --force       # overwrite even if new graph has fewer nodes\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --graph path/to/graph.json  # custom graph location\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --resolution 1.5            # more, smaller communities\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --exclude-hubs 99           # exclude p99 degree nodes from partitioning\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --no-label                  # keep \"Community N\" placeholders\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --backend=gemini            # backend for community naming\ngraphify cluster-only ./my-project --backend=gemini --model gemini-2.5-pro  # specific model\ngraphify label ./my-project                                    # (re)name communities with the configured backend\ngraphify label ./my-project --backend=openai --model gpt-4o   # force a specific backend and model\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCommunity names:\u003c/strong\u003e inside an agent (Claude Code, Gemini CLI) the agent names communities itself. When you run the bare CLI, \u003ccode\u003ecluster-only\u003c/code\u003e auto-names them with the configured backend (built-in or custom OpenAI-compatible provider) — pass \u003ccode\u003e--no-label\u003c/code\u003e to keep \u003ccode\u003eCommunity N\u003c/code\u003e, or run \u003ccode\u003egraphify label\u003c/code\u003e to (re)generate names on demand.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eLearn href=\"https://graphifylabs.ai\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePenpax\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e is the always-on layer built on top of graphify — it applies the same graph approach to your entire working life: meetings, browser history, emails, files, and code, updating continuously in the background.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBuilt for people whose work lives across hundreds of conversations and documents they can never fully reconstruct. No cloud, fully on-device.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFree trial launching soon.\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ca href=\"https://graphifylabs.ai\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eJoin the waitlist →\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eContributing\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eDevelopment setup\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-development-setup\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Development setup\" href=\"#development-setup\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe project uses \u003ca href=\"https://docs.astral.sh/uv/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003euv\u003c/a\u003e for dev workflow. Install it once, then:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"git clone https://github.com/safishamsi/graphify.git\ncd graphify\ngit checkout v8                        # active development branch\n\n# Create the project venv and install graphify + all extras + the dev group\n# (pytest). uv installs the dev dependency group by default; pass --no-dev to\n# skip it.\nuv sync --all-extras\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egit clone https://github.com/safishamsi/graphify.git\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e graphify\ngit checkout v8                        \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e active development branch\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Create the project venv and install graphify + all extras + the dev group\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e (pytest). uv installs the dev dependency group by default; pass --no-dev to\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e skip it.\u003c/span\u003e\nuv sync --all-extras\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eVerify the editable install:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"uv run graphify --version\nuv run python -c \u0026quot;import graphify; print(graphify.__file__)\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003euv run graphify --version\nuv run python -c \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eimport graphify; print(graphify.__file__)\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eRunning tests\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-running-tests\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Running tests\" href=\"#running-tests\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"uv run pytest tests/ -q                # run the full suite\nuv run pytest tests/test_extract.py -q # one module\nuv run pytest tests/ -q -k \u0026quot;python\u0026quot;    # filter by name\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003euv run pytest tests/ -q                \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e run the full suite\u003c/span\u003e\nuv run pytest tests/test_extract.py -q \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e one module\u003c/span\u003e\nuv run pytest tests/ -q -k \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003epython\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e filter by name\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emacOS note: the test suite includes both \u003ccode\u003esample.f90\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003esample.F90\u003c/code\u003e fixtures. These collide on case-insensitive HFS+ / APFS file systems. Run on Linux or in a Docker container if you need to test both Fortran variants simultaneously.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 branch.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCommit style: \u003ccode\u003efix: \u0026lt;description\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003efeat: \u0026lt;description\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003edocs: \u0026lt;description\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBefore opening a PR, run \u003ccode\u003euv run pytest tests/ -q\u003c/code\u003e and confirm it passes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdd a fixture file to \u003ccode\u003etests/fixtures/\u003c/code\u003e and tests to \u003ccode\u003etests/test_languages.py\u003c/code\u003e for any new language extractor.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat to dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWorked examples\u003c/strong\u003e are the most useful contribution. Run \u003ccode\u003e/graphify\u003c/code\u003e on a real corpus, save the output to \u003ccode\u003eworked/{slug}/\u003c/code\u003e, write an honest \u003ccode\u003ereview.md\u003c/code\u003e covering what the graph got right and wrong, and open a PR.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExtraction bugs\u003c/strong\u003e — open an issue with the input file, the cache entry (\u003ccode\u003egraphify-out/cache/\u003c/code\u003e), and what was missed or wrong.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca href=\"/safishamsi/graphify/blob/v8/ARCHITECTURE.md\"\u003eARCHITECTURE.md\u003c/a\u003e for module responsibilities and how to add a language.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003c/article\u003e","loaded":true,"timedOut":false,"errorMessage":null,"headerInfo":{"toc":[{"level":2,"text":"Prerequisites","anchor":"prerequisites","htmlText":"Prerequisites"},{"level":2,"text":"Install","anchor":"install","htmlText":"Install"},{"level":3,"text":"Pick <p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a href="https://star-history.com/#safishamsi/graphify&amp;Date" rel="nofollow">
    <img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/4d6f410013f7579cfd95d11c62d8f5da3bd7a8c7502b58f5ee7f4218bed4989f/68747470733a2f2f6170692e737461722d686973746f72792e636f6d2f7376673f7265706f733d736166697368616d73692f677261706869667926747970653d44617465" alt="Star History Chart" width="370" data-canonical-src="https://api.star-history.com/svg?repos=safishamsi/graphify&amp;type=Date" style="max-width: 100%;">
  </a>

<p dir="auto">Type <code>/graphify</code> in your AI coding assistant and it maps your entire project — code, docs, PDFs, images, videos — into a knowledge graph you can query instead of grepping through files.</p>
<p dir="auto">Works in Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode, Kilo Code, Cursor, Gemini CLI, GitHub Copilot CLI, VS Code Copilot Chat, Aider, Amp, OpenClaw, Factory Droid, Trae, Hermes, Kimi Code, Kiro, Pi, Devin CLI, and Google Antigravity.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/graphify ."><pre class="notranslate"><code>/graphify .
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">That's it. You get three files:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="graphify-out/
├── graph.html       open in any browser — click nodes, filter, search
├── GRAPH_REPORT.md  the highlights: key concepts, surprising connections, suggested questions
└── graph.json       the full graph — query it anytime without re-reading your files"><pre class="notranslate"><code>graphify-out/
├── graph.html       open in any browser — click nodes, filter, search
├── GRAPH_REPORT.md  the highlights: key concepts, surprising connections, suggested questions
└── graph.json       the full graph — query it anytime without re-reading your files
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For a readable architecture page with Mermaid call-flow diagrams, run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="graphify export callflow-html"><pre>graphify <span class="pl-k">export</span> callflow-html</pre></div>
<hr>

<th>Requirement</th>
<th>Minimum</th>
<th>Check</th>
<th>Install</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Python</td>
<td>3.10+</td>
<td><code>python --version</code></td>
<td><a href="https://www.python.org/downloads/" rel="nofollow">python.org</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>uv <em>(recommended)</em></td>
<td>any</td>
<td><code>uv --version</code></td>
<td><code>curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>pipx <em>(alternative)</em></td>
<td>any</td>
<td><code>pipx --version</code></td>
<td><code>pip install pipx</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>macOS quick install (Homebrew):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="brew install python@3.12 uv"><pre>brew install python@3.12 uv</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Windows quick install:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="winget install astral-sh.uv"><pre>winget install astral<span class="pl-k">-</span>sh.uv</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Ubuntu/Debian:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sudo apt install python3.12 python3-pip pipx
# or install uv:
curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh"><pre>sudo apt install python3.12 python3-pip pipx
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or install uv:</span>
curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh</pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install</h2><a id="user-content-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install" href="#install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Official package:</strong> The PyPI package is <code>graphifyy</code> (double-y). Other <code>graphify*</code> packages on PyPI are not affiliated. The CLI command is still <code>graphify</code>.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Step 1 — install the package:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Recommended (uv puts graphify on PATH automatically):
uv tool install graphifyy

# Alternatives:
pipx install graphifyy
pip install graphifyy  # may need PATH setup — see note below"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Recommended (uv puts graphify on PATH automatically):</span>
uv tool install graphifyy

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Alternatives:</span>
pipx install graphifyy
pip install graphifyy  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> may need PATH setup — see note below</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Step 2 — register the skill with your AI assistant:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="graphify install"><pre>graphify install</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">That's it. Open your AI assistant and type <code>/graphify .</code></p>
<p dir="auto">To install the assistant skill into the current repository instead of your user
profile, add <code>--project</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="graphify install --project
graphify install --project --platform codex"><pre>graphify install --project
graphify install --project --platform codex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Project-scoped installs write under the current directory, for example
<code>.claude/skills/graphify/SKILL.md</code> or <code>.agents/skills/graphify/SKILL.md</code> (plus a
<code>references/</code> sidecar the skill loads on demand), and
print a <code>git add</code> hint for files that can be committed.
Per-platform commands that support project-scoped installs accept the same flag,
for example <code>graphify claude install --project</code> or <code>graphify codex install --project</code>.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>PowerShell note:</strong> Use <code>graphify .</code> not <code>/graphify .</code> — the leading slash is a path separator in PowerShell.</p>
</blockquote>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong><code>graphify: command not found</code>?</strong> Use <code>uv tool install graphifyy</code> or <code>pipx install graphifyy</code> — both put the CLI on PATH automatically. With plain <code>pip</code>, add <code>~/.local/bin</code> (Linux) or <code>~/Library/Python/3.x/bin</code> (Mac) to your PATH, or run <code>python -m graphify</code>.</p>
</blockquote>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Avoid <code>pip install</code> on Mac/Windows</strong> if possible. The skill resolves Python at runtime from <code>graphify-out/.graphify_python</code>; if that points to a different environment than where <code>pip</code> installed the package, you'll get <code>ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'graphify'</code>. <code>uv tool install</code> and <code>pipx install</code> isolate the package in their own env and avoid this entirely.</p>
</blockquote>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Git hooks and uv tool / pipx:</strong> <code>graphify hook install</code> embeds the current interpreter path directly into the hook scripts at install time, so the post-commit hook fires correctly even in GUI git clients and CI runners where <code>~/.local/bin</code> is not on PATH. If you reinstall or upgrade graphify, re-run <code>graphify hook install</code> to refresh the embedded path.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Pick your platform</h3><a id="user-content-pick-your-platform" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Pick your platform" href="#pick-your-platform"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">Codex users also need <code>multi_agent = true</code> under <code>[features]</code> in <code>~/.codex/config.toml</code> for parallel extraction. CodeBuddy uses the same Agent tool and PreToolUse hook mechanism as Claude Code. Factory Droid uses the <code>Task</code> tool for parallel subagent dispatch. OpenClaw and Aider use sequential extraction (parallel agent support is still early on those platforms). Trae uses the Agent tool for parallel subagent dispatch and does <strong>not</strong> support PreToolUse hooks — AGENTS.md is the always-on mechanism.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Codex uses <code>$graphify</code> instead of <code>/graphify</code>.</p>
</blockquote>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Make your assistant always use the graph</h2><a id="user-content-make-your-assistant-always-use-the-graph" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Make your assistant always use the graph" href="#make-your-assistant-always-use-the-graph"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
